# GreySynth Newly Simplified MBA Wins (`ec490c7` Paper Release -> `3f856ab` `proof-coverage`)

This document lists **every expression across the full 8-dataset MBA audit (7,011 rows, zero `--limit`) that previously did NOT simplify to ground truth in the ACM CCS 2026 paper release (`ec490c7`) and NOW simplifies cleanly to `output_size <= ground_truth_size` with 100% independent proof replay (`mba_proof` / `mba_equiv`)** on `proof-coverage` (`3f856ab`), plus the top 25 largest collapses on `C-Full` / Tigress L5.

> **Plain-number formatting:** All 64-bit hex bitvector literals (and `<64>` width suffixes) are rendered as plain signed 64-bit decimal integers (e.g., `0xfffffffffffffffd<64>` -> `-3`, `0x0000000000000457<64>` -> `1111`) so outputs can be visually compared directly against the dataset ground truth.

## Summary: Newly Solved Expressions (`<= GT`, Proved Now, Not Solved Before)

Across all **7,011** raw expressions, solved (`<= GT` & independently proved) outputs jumped from **1,025** (`ec490c7`) to **4,756** (`3f856ab`) — including **3,827 newly solved clear wins** that previously failed, timed out, were refuted, or remained obfuscated above ground truth.

| Dataset | Total Rows | Solved Before (`ec490c7`) | Solved Now (`3f856ab`) | **Newly Solved Wins (Listed Below)** | Already Solved in Both |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| **PLDI Non-Poly** | 1,003 | 6 | **559** | **553** | 6 |
| **PLDI Poly** | 1,008 | 1 | **600** | **599** | 1 |
| **MBA-Obfuscator Nonlinear** | 1,000 | 0 | **691** | **691** | 0 |
| **MBA-Obfuscator Linear** | 1,000 | 10 | **680** | **670** | 10 |
| **msimba (64-bit Masked MBA)** | 1,000 | 0 | **1,000** | **1,000** | 0 |
| **QSynth EA** | 500 | 152 | **252** | **121** | 131 |
| **Loki** | 1,000 | 680 | **776** | **149** | 627 |
| **Full-Loki (Raw)** | 500 | 176 | **198** | **44** | 154 |
| **Total** | **7,011** | **1,025** | **4,756** | **3,827** | **929** |
